一、 美國—內需與房市提供支撐 美國財政及通膨風險升高 
2026 年 9 月美國零售與餐飲服務銷售額經季節性調整後為 7,450 億美元，較
上月增加 0.8%，年增 6.3%。若扣除汽車及零件銷售，零售額月增 0.9%；進一步
扣除汽車、零件及加油站後，核心零售銷售月增 0.2%，顯示整體消費需求仍維持
成長。從主要業別表現觀察，加油站、非實體零售商及餐飲服務與飲酒場所銷售
額均較上月增加，其中加油站及餐飲消費表現相對亮眼，電子及家電商店銷售亦
呈成長。整體而言，9 月美國零售銷售延續擴張走勢，顯示民間消費仍具支撐力
道，且多數主要消費類別維持正成長，惟核心零售增幅較前月放緩，後續仍須關
注物價、就業及金融情勢變化對消費支出的影響。 
2026 年 9 月(2026 財年第 12 個月)美國聯邦政府財政赤字為 269.0 億美元，較
去年同期 1,979.4 億美元財政盈餘轉為赤字。當月政府收入 5,578.8 億美元，年增
2.6%；政府支出 5,847.8 億美元，年增 69.1%，支出增幅明顯高於收入。收入方
面，9 月關稅淨收入由 8 月的 128.4 億美元降至 59.1 億美元，累計 2026 財年關稅
淨收入約 1,732.2 億美元，年減 11.1%，關稅退稅增加已部分抵銷關稅收入成長。
支出方面，9 月聯邦政府淨利息支出約 347.9 億美元，全年累計達 1 兆 517.5 億美
元，年增 8.4%，反映高利率環境下債務利息負擔持續攀升。累計 2026 財年全年，
美國聯邦政府財政赤字約 1 兆 9,924.7 億美元，較 2025 財年擴大 12.2%。其中，
政府收入年增 3.2%至 5 兆 4,033.3 億美元，支出年增 5.5%至 7 兆 3,958.0 億美元，
顯示支出成長仍快於收入增加。從支出結構觀察，社會安全、醫療保險、國防及
淨利息支出均較上年增加，持續推升財政壓力。整體而言，美國財政赤字擴大主
要反映政府支出成長強勁、利息負擔居高不下，以及關稅退稅增加削弱部分收入
效果。在聯邦債務總額突破 40 兆美元背景下，未來財政赤字走勢、債務融資成本
及財政永續性問題，仍將是市場關注的重要焦點。 
2026 年 9 月美國成屋銷售經季節調整後年率升至 399 萬戶，較上月增加 1.5%，
惟較去年同期減少 2.2%，整體房市成交動能仍偏疲弱。區域表現方面，南部及西
部分別月增 2.8%，中西部持平，東北部月減 2.1%，四大地區銷售量均較去年同
期下滑。房價方面，成屋售價中位數為 41.75 萬美元，年增 1.3%，已連續 39 個

機票、休閒娛樂及服飾等價格上漲，通訊價格則下跌。整體而言，9 月美國通膨
明顯回升，主要受汽油價格大幅上漲及住房成本續增帶動；核心 CPI 月增率維持
0.3%，年增率則回升至 2.6%，顯示核心物價壓力較前月略有增強，未來通膨走勢
仍須持續關注能源價格變化及住房等服務項目價格表現。 
 2026 年 9 月美國生產者物價指數(PPI)經季節調整後月增 1.3%，高於上月之
0.5%，年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%。其中，最終需求商品價格月增 2.6%，主要受
能源價格月增 10.8%帶動，以汽油、柴油及航空燃油價格漲幅較為顯著；食品價
格亦月增 1.2%。另一方面，最終需求服務價格月增 0.8%，主要反映批發及零售
利潤率、運輸服務價格上升。剔除食品、能源及貿易服務後之核心最終需求 PPI
月增 0.5%，高於上月之 0.3%，年增率由 4.8%升至 5.0%。整體而言，9 月 PPI 升
幅明顯擴大，主要反映能源與服務價格同步上揚，顯示生產端成本壓力仍偏高，
後續仍須關注能源價格及運輸成本變化對通膨走勢之影響。

通膨預期持續惡化，聯準會可能必須採取更積極的緊縮措施，以維持物價穩定。

投資熱潮支撐而維持韌性，但能源價格上漲及 AI 投資擴張恐推升通膨壓力，並
未對下次利率決策預作表態。整體而言，聯準會官員發言顯示政策立場仍偏鷹派，
但對短期升息時點保持彈性，未來通膨及通膨預期變化仍是利率決策的重要依據。 
近期美墨貿易談判再度受到關稅爭議影響。據《華爾街日報》報導，雙方原
已接近達成初步協議，美國擬調降對墨西哥汽車、鋼鐵及鋁製品的部分進口關稅，
以換取墨西哥在貿易政策、商品採購及其他經貿議題上的讓步，並為後續《美墨
加協定》（USMCA）檢討與延長協商創造有利條件。然而，美國商務部長 Howard 
Lutnick 反對大幅調降目前對墨西哥汽車課徵的 25%關稅，以及鋼鐵、鋁製品與部
分衍生製成品的 50%關稅，使談判進展受阻。墨西哥方面則希望將汽車關稅降至
15%或以下，以縮小與日本、南韓汽車適用稅率的差距，並擴大汽車關稅抵減機
制對美墨加三國零組件價值的認列範圍；惟美方仍須考量國內金屬產業對降稅可
能衝擊本土生產及就業的疑慮。儘管雙方對關稅調整幅度仍存分歧，墨西哥經貿
官員表示協商已有部分進展，美國商務部亦強調持續與墨方保持溝通，顯示談判
尚未正式破局。整體而言，美墨關稅協商結果不僅攸關北美汽車及金屬產業供應
鏈成本與競爭力，也可能影響 USMCA 後續檢討與延長協商進程；若雙方遲遲無
法縮小分歧，恐增加企業投資與供應鏈布局的不確定性，並對北美區域貿易整合
形成壓力。 
二、歐元區—工業生產走疲，前景有待觀察 
 歐元區 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)較 7 月下滑 0.5%，呈現連續三個月下降，
其中非耐久消費財、中間財及能源產出分別減少 2.7%、0.8%、0.6%，僅資本財、
耐久消費財產出分別成長 0.4%、0.2%。儘管德國 8 月工業生產(不含營造業)反彈
0.4%，係受惠車廠陸續結束歲修，但法、義、西工業生產分別下跌 0.3%、1.3%、
0.8%為主要拖累因素，反映先前美伊戰爭帶來的庫存儲備需求逐漸消退，尤其以
能源密集產業(化工、造紙、鋼鐵等)變化最為明顯。整體而言，即便排除能源密集
產業的波動，其他製造業諸如製藥、汽車、機械等生產情形皆偏疲軟。 
 展望未來，近期德國在財政擴張的支持下，工廠訂單大幅成長，未來可望陸
續反映在該國工業生產表現；但法國政治、選舉等不利前景重挫市場信心，恐會
影響企業以及政府在各項支出、投資決策，進而形成拖累因素。另外，歐盟的「下
一代歐盟復甦計畫」(NextGenerationEU)已在 9 月底截止撥款申請，並將在年底前

三、日本—AI 投資續強，帶動機械訂單穩健復甦 
 8 月核心機械訂單(扣除波動訂單)月增 5.2%，其中製造業訂單月增 1.6%，人
工智慧相關投資仍是主要驅動力，資通訊產業訂單月增 13.0%，有色金屬也大幅
反彈；汽車產業月增 13.1%，並已連續四個月成長，顯示投資需求穩健。非製造
業訂單(扣除波動訂單)月增 2.9%，其中租賃業成長尤為強勁；運輸業、建築業在
7 月大幅成長後出現回落；零售業則在連續三個月下滑後月增 29.2%。整體而言，
機械訂單數據顯示企業資本支出呈現更廣泛且穩固的復甦趨勢，尤以 AI 相關投
資為主要支撐。 
四、中國—AI 相關需求帶動出口動能強勁成長 
9 月出口年增率較 8 月大幅增加 11.3 個百分點至 36.3%，出口總值為 4,472
億美元；累計前九個月出口年增率上升為 21.3%，累積出口總值為 33,707 億美元。
前九個月出口主要成長項目(累積年增率)包含 IC(108.1%)、自動數據處理設備(包
含伺服器等，54.6%)、船舶(29.9%)、汽車(54.3%)，其中 IC 及自動數據處理設備
出口加速成長，為 9 月出口年增率大幅上升的主要因素。 
9 月進口年增率較 8 月增 2.8 個百分點至 31%，進口總值為 3,137 億美元；累
計前九個月進口值成長 27.5%至 24,326 億美元。其中 IC、自動數據處理設備(包
含伺服器等)進口分別成長 63.5%、124.8%，呼應中國對高階 IC 的依賴，以及部
分伺服器關鍵組件有賴於進口滿足所需。進出口相抵後，前九個月貿易順差達
9,381 億美元，僅較去年同期增加 7.7%，增幅明顯低於出口，顯示出口擴張同時
伴隨關鍵原物料進口大幅增加，稀釋出口成長的加值效果，亦反映部分高附加價
值產品依賴國際供應鏈支持。 
9 月生產者通膨(出廠價格)由 8 月的 3.8%升至 5%，主因原油價格自 7 月起走
升，其影響逐漸反映於上游生產成本。當月石油和天然氣開採業出廠價格年增率
續揚 14.1 個百分點至 24.5%，並帶動燃料加工業出廠價格年增率上揚至 21.6%，
此外，對於原油價格波動較為敏感的化學品、化纖出廠價格年增率加速升至
12.1%、12.3%，顯示原油價格上漲的影響開始擴散至中下游產業。此外，由於近
期鋼鐵價格走弱，黑色金屬礦產開採、冶煉加工業出廠價格分別年減 3.4%、0.2%，

為主要拖累因素。另因金價盤整，有色金屬礦採選業出廠價格年增率亦持續放慢。 
9 月消費者通膨(CPI)升 0.1 個百分點為 0.9%，但仍然是今年以來第三低的水
準，核心通膨(不含食品及能源)持平於 1 %不變。該月燃油通膨續增 6.6 個百分點
至 14.9%，在客運成本增加之下，旅遊服務通膨亦增加 0.8 個百分點至 3.5%，此

力之下，企業仍大多自行吸收成本，加上政府壓抑零售燃油及民生水電，故目前
原油價格高漲的影響尚未反映於消費端價格。惟考量能源成本傳導存在時間差，
若油價維持高檔或進一步上漲，未來通膨仍有進一步上升的風險。 
展望未來，歐中談判已就汽車貿易達成部分共識，包含未來中國出口至歐盟
的混合動力汽車設定每年 35 萬輛的配額上限，且配額規模可逐年增加 6%。依照
歐盟預估 2026 年自中國進口約 80 萬輛混合動力汽車推算，中國對歐盟的混合動
力車出口量將較原先潛在規模減少一半以上。雖該協議不利於汽車出口，但有助
於避免歐盟進一步擴大貿易保護措施，中國車廠則可能會加速透過赴歐投資設
廠、提升當地生產比重等方式因應配額限制，藉此維持在歐洲市場的競爭力與市
占率。 
五、印度及東南亞—IMF 上調印度及東協的經濟展望；新、馬第三季 GDP 均逾

(1)據 IMF2026 年 10 月經濟展望報告(WEO)(詳下表)，印度仍是全球主要經濟體
中成長最快者之一，雖然面對油價上漲及全球不確定性，但內需及民間消費韌性
強勁，服務業持續擴張，2026、2027 年 GDP 成長率預估皆為 7.0%。東協國家方
面，受惠於 AI 伺服器與資料中心投資、科技產業供應鏈重組、美中貿易重組帶
來轉單效應等利多，越南、馬來西亞、新加坡、菲律賓及泰國均為 AI 供應鏈受益
國，但須留意地緣政治、全球貿易重組及 AI 投資循環變化，帶來的通膨及經濟
下行風險，建議各國政府提升農業生產效率及糧食安全，提升數位經濟及服務業
競爭力，強化財政紀律並提高公共投資效率，維持穩健貨幣政策與匯率穩定。

5.2%，以批發貿易受電子出口帶動表現尤佳。IMF 預期新加坡將持續受惠於 AI、

六、台灣—AI 帶動出口、投資與消費成長，中經院上修 2026 年經濟預測 
中經院 10 月 16 日公布最新預測，上修 2026 年台灣經濟成長率至 12.16%，
創 1988 年以來新高，主要受惠於 AI 技術快速發展帶動全球科技投資，半導體與
資通訊產品需求強勁，出口維持高速成長，同時民間投資與消費亦同步擴張，經
濟成長動能由內、外需共同支撐。另一方面，由於中東地緣政治風險升溫帶動油
價維持高檔，能源價格外溢效果推升整體物價，中經院將 2026 年 CPI 年增率預
估上修至 2.06%，惟整體通膨仍屬相對溫和。

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
經濟 
⚫ 2026 年第 2 季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值上修至 2.2%，惟低於第 1 季
之 2.5%。成長主要由個人消費及固定投資帶動，其中消費貢獻 2.51 個
百分點；惟進口大增 12.6%，致淨出口拖累 1.10 個百分點。另實質私人
國內最終銷售成長 4.6%，顯示內需仍具韌性。 
⚫ 9 月美國非農就業僅增 2.9 萬人，遠低於前月及近一年平均，失業率升
至 4.2%，薪資增幅續降，顯示企業聘僱動能轉弱。惟勞動市場尚未明顯
惡化，後續仍須關注就業降溫對聯準會利率決策之影響。 
⚫ 8 月美國職位空缺數由 733.5 萬個降至 707.9 萬個，職缺率降至 4.3%，
顯示企業徵才需求略為降溫；惟聘僱人數增至 519.2 萬人。主動離職及
裁員規模同步下降，反映勞工轉職意願與企業人力調整趨於審慎，勞動
市場供需大致維持平衡。 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數為 54.5，連續第九個月維持擴張。
新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示需求仍具韌性；惟生產、出
口及進口動能略為放緩，且原物料成本持續攀升，加上關稅政策、地緣
政治及供應鏈不確定性仍存，製造業仍面臨部分壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國 PCE 物價指數月增 0.3%、年增 3.4%，核心 PCE 月增 0.2%、
年增 3.0%，顯示通膨大致持穩但短期物價壓力略升。另一方面，個人消
費支出月增 0.9%、實質消費成長 0.6%，帶動儲蓄率降至 4.1%，顯示家
庭需求仍具韌性；後續須關注消費熱度及通膨對聯準會政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期聯準會官員立場趨於分歧，理事 Michael Barr 認為能源價格及 AI
投資熱潮可能延緩通膨降溫，未來仍可能進一步升息；惟 John Williams、
Philip Jefferson 及 Michelle Bowman 等官員則主張暫無急於升息的必要，
傾向觀察後續經濟數據。加以 9 月非農就業明顯放緩，市場對 10 月升
息預期大幅降溫，焦點轉向 12 月會議。 
⚫ 台積電傳評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作赴德州設廠，雙方仍處討論階
段。合作可能由台積電主導營運，或由 Terafab 持多數股權、台積電提
供技術。若成真，將擴大台積電美國布局，並支援 Tesla、SpaceX 及 xAI
晶片需求。

兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處

一、 美國—美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
2026 年第二季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值由前次估計之 1.5%上修至 2.2%，
惟仍低於第一季之 2.5%，顯示經濟持續擴張但成長動能略有放緩。此次上修主要
反映投資、消費及政府支出優於原先估計。就成長結構觀察，個人消費支出仍為
主要動能，貢獻 2.51 個百分點，其中服務及商品消費分別貢獻 1.57 及 0.94 個百
分點；私人投資貢獻 0.82 個百分點，以設備投資及智慧財產權投資表現強勁，惟
庫存調整拖累 0.53 個百分點。對外貿易方面，出口成長 5.0%，但進口大幅增加
12.6%，致淨出口拖累 GDP 成長 1.10 個百分點。另反映內需基本面的實質私人國
內最終銷售成長 4.6%，高於第一季之 1.8%，顯示民間需求明顯增強。物價方面，
第 2 季 PCE 及核心 PCE 物價指數分別上升 5.0%及 3.3%，顯示通膨壓力仍偏高。
整體而言，美國經濟仍由消費與固定投資支撐，惟進口快速增加及通膨黏著性，
仍為後續經濟前景的重要觀察重點。 
2026 年 9 月美國非農就業人數增加 2.9 萬人，明顯低於上月修正後的 13.3 萬
人，也低於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.5 萬人的水準；失業率小幅升至 4.2%，
整體仍維持在 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%的區間，反映勞動市場整體變化有限，惟新
增就業力道已轉趨疲弱。家庭調查方面，勞動參與率升至 61.8%，就業人口比為
59.2%，均較上月小幅上升；長期失業人數約 194.4 萬人，占總失業人口 27.1%，
大致維持穩定。產業別方面，主要產業就業變化有限，其中醫療保健增加 1.7 萬
人、營建業增加 1.1 萬人、製造業增加 0.9 萬人，金融活動則減少 0.7 萬人，政府
部門減少 1.7 萬人。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升至 37.81 美元，月增 0.1%、
年增 3.0%，平均每週工時維持 34.4 小時。另 7 月與 8 月新增就業合計下修 6 萬
人，反映前二月就業動能較初估疲弱。整體而言，9 月美國新增就業明顯放緩、
失業率小幅上升且薪資增速降溫，企業聘僱動能較先前轉弱；惟失業率仍維持於
近期區間，勞動市場尚未出現明顯惡化跡象，後續仍須關注企業聘僱需求是否持
續降溫，以及對聯準會利率決策的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings)由上月修正後之 733.5 萬個減少
25.6 萬個至 707.9 萬個，職缺率由 4.4%降至 4.3%，顯示企業用人需求略有降溫；

惟聘僱人數由 514.6 萬人小幅增加至 519.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.2%升至 3.3%。總離
職人數由 512.8 萬人減少至 507.0 萬人，離職率維持於 3.2%。就離職結構觀察，
主動離職(Quits)人數由 308.9 萬人降至 306.6 萬人，主動離職率維持 1.9%；裁員
與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges)人數則由 170.2 萬人降至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由
1.1%降至 1.0%。產業別方面，職位空缺增加主要來自零售業、金融保險業、資訊
業及休閒餐飲業；聘僱人數則以政府部門及製造業增幅較為明顯。整體而言，8 月
美國職缺數較前月下滑，反映企業徵才需求略有降溫，但聘僱活動仍維持穩定，
且主動離職與裁員規模同步下降，顯示勞工轉職意願及企業人力調整趨於審慎，
勞動市場供需仍大致維持平衡。 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 為 54.5，
較上月之 54.6 微降 0.1 點，惟已連續第九個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，顯示製
造業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由 53.7 升至 55.3，需求動能續增；生
產指數由 58.3 降至 56.7，雖仍維持擴張，但成長速度略有放緩；就業指數由 51.2
升至 52.7，反映企業聘僱需求持續增加。供應商交貨指數由 59.3 微降至 59.0，交
貨延遲情況仍存；庫存指數則由 50.6 降至 48.6，企業備貨態度轉趨保守。此外，
價格指數由 71.1 升至 77.9，原物料及投入成本壓力升高；未完成訂單指數由 51.8
升至 56.4，訂單去化變慢。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由 53.2 降至 50.9，進口指
數亦由 52.5 降至 51.0，外需成長動能及企業備料需求略為放緩。整體而言，9 月
美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示製造
業景氣仍具韌性；惟出口及進口增速放緩、投入成本壓力升高，加上關稅政策、
地緣政治風險及供應鏈不確定性仍存，後續發展仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 8 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增率由上月之 0.1%升至 0.3%，
年增率維持於 3.4%；扣除食品及能源後之核心 PCE 月增率由 0.1%升至 0.2%，年
增率亦持平於 3.0%，顯示整體通膨大致持穩，惟短期物價漲勢較前月略有回升。
另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)月增 0.3%，實質可支配所得持
平；個人消費支出月增 0.9%，實質個人消費支出成長 0.6%，顯示民間消費表現
明顯回溫。由於消費增幅高於所得增幅，個人儲蓄率由前月之 4.6%降至 4.1%。
整體而言，8 月美國通膨表現大致持穩，但消費支出顯著回升，反映家庭需求仍
具韌性；後續仍須持續關注消費熱度、通膨走勢及其對聯準會貨幣政策路徑之影
響。

近期聯準會官員談話呈現鷹鴿分歧。聯準會理事 Michael Barr 認為，中東局
勢推升能源價格，以及 AI 相關投資熱潮帶動需求增長，均可能阻礙通膨回落至
2%目標，未來仍不排除進一步升息的必要性；惟 紐約聯準銀行總裁 John Williams、
聯準會副主席 Philip Jefferson 及金融監理副主席 Michelle Bowman 則強調，在 9
月升息後，當前並無急迫性立即進一步收緊政策，應先觀察後續通膨、就業及金
融市場數據變化，再評估是否有進一步調整利率的必要。受官員談話轉趨審慎，
以及 9 月非農就業表現疲弱影響，市場對 10 月 FOMC 會議再度升息的預期明顯
降溫，政策焦點逐漸轉向 12 月會議及年底前是否仍有一次升息空間。 
晶圓代工龍頭台積電傳出正評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作，在美國德州布
局晶圓廠，馬斯克也於社群平台 X 證實雙方正在討論，並表示「目前僅在討論階
段，但可能會有進展」。目前浮上檯面的合作模式包括由台積電持有並營運新廠，
SpaceX 或 Terafab 透過入股及承諾採購晶圓參與；另一方案則由 Terafab 掌握多
數股權，台積電提供製程技術與晶圓廠營運經驗。Terafab 旨在因應 Tesla、SpaceX
及 xAI 快速成長的 AI 晶片需求，首期投資規模達 168 億美元。若合作成真，不
僅可望成為台積電繼亞利桑那州後在美國的新布局，也凸顯先進製程與穩定晶片
供應的重要性，但目前具體合作方式、時程與投資內容仍未正式定案。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨、核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1 個百分
點至 3.8%、2.5%，主因國際原油價格上漲，使能源通膨上升 4.5 個百分點至 18.8%，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品通膨 0.3 個百分點至 1.4%。另外，服
務通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 3.2%，近三個月服務通膨大致呈現橫向波動，部分反
映旅遊旺季因素影響。基於布蘭特原油持續位於每桶 90 美元以上，預期年底前
仍將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管
道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含：(1)歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%，失業人數增加 2.6 萬人，
主要是義大利勞動參與人數增加所致，不過，基於 PMI 顯示近期德國勞動雇用需
求持續增溫，將有助於穩定勞動市場表現；(2)歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2
個百分點至 14.2%，顯示通膨壓力削弱民眾購買力，致儲蓄暫時性減少；(3)歐元
區第二季房價年增率為 4%，較第一季降 0.6 個百分點，主因法國中古屋面臨嚴格

環保法規，須負擔高昂翻修成本，使法國房價呈現連三季下滑所拖累。 
 近一週法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大
至 125 個基點以上，主要反映市場對法國主權公債可持續性疑慮升溫。儘管目前
政府計畫將 2027 年財政赤字縮限至 5%，惟政府在國會並無穩定多數席次，即使
能以行政命令強行通過預算，也可能面臨倒閣風險；此外，市場對於 2027 年 4 月
總統大選後，新總統能否取得國會足夠支持，推動削減赤字與控制債務的改革亦
缺乏信心。因此未來法國仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，
暫時不利於投資及消費信心。 
三、日本—景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%，主要係受到該月地震及豪雨等天然災害影響，導致
部分工廠暫時停產。從細項來看，汽車業受停工影響最為明顯，產量下滑 6.8%，
電子產品產量亦減少 1.9%。惟生產機械產量連續三個月成長，加上核心資本財出
貨持續增加，顯示企業投資需求仍相當強勁，有助於支撐後續工業生產回升。從
企業預期來看，製造商預期 9 月產量將成長 3.2%，顯示生產活動可望迅速回溫，
在供應鏈逐步恢復、庫存回補需求持續，以及穩健的企業資本支出支撐下，預計
第三季工業生產仍可維持正成長，且成長動能可望延續至年底。 
 8 月零售月減 1.2%，雖然表現略優於市場預期，但仍顯示民間消費動能正在
放緩。此次疲弱主要原因來自耐久財銷售下滑，其中家電銷售因空調等產品在換
機需求告一段落後明顯回落，汽車銷售也隨政府刺激措施效應逐漸消退而連續第
四個月下降。相較之下，食品、服裝等非耐久財銷售雖同步走弱，但降幅相對有
限，反映薪資成長仍對消費提供部分支撐。惟不利天候與通膨壓力仍是主要風險，
8 月至 9 月持續豪雨已影響服裝等非必需消費，而除米價以外的食品及商品價格
持續上漲，也正侵蝕家庭實質購買力，恐限制消費者信心與消費復甦力道。 
 9 月東京核心通膨年增率升至 2.7%，雖然東京都免費托兒及免費水費政策造
成的基期效應貢獻約 0.5 個百分點，但通膨升溫主因仍是價格上漲已由能源與食
品擴散至整體經濟。受石油價格上揚帶動，企業開始加速將原物料及薪資成本轉
嫁給消費者，帶動商品與服務價格普遍走高；租金通膨更升至 2%，創 1994 年以
來新高，公共交通及醫療等項目也開始調漲。隨著東京的物價壓力逐步反映至全
國數據，日本央行進一步升息的可能性升高。

用相關內容。

8

四、中國—為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
9 月官方製造業 PMI 上升 0.3 點至 50.1，為近三個月高點，新訂單、出口訂
單指數分別為 50.5、50，並帶動生產指數上升至 51.7，不過，庫存指數下降、就
業指數加速衰退、企業展望減弱，皆拖累 PMI 指數水準。此外，投入、產出物價
指數分別上升至 60.8、54，顯示成本壓力加劇，但售價調整速度依然較為遲緩，
不利於獲利表現。官方非製造業 PMI 上升 1.2 點至 50.2，其中建築業活動指數大
增 3.4 點至 50.3，脫離連續八個月的衰退區間，主要是政府加大財政刺激力度，
推動重大建設開工；非製造業的從業人員指數為 45.9，顯示就業情況仍顯嚴峻，
亦較不利於內需表現。 
鑒於內需動能疲弱，人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是
將資金引導至政府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍，
將水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通信網、城市地下管網及物流網等建設納入
支持，有助於提振內需動能。未來若內需復甦力道不如預期，不排除人行將進一
步降準或擴大結構性貨幣工具支持經濟；至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及
中美利差等因素，可能性相對較低。 
五、印度及東南亞—泰國、菲律賓出口均達 2 成以上的高速成長

增率升至 2.2%，連續 3 個月上升並創 2 年新高。受全球能源價格居高不下及氣候
條件不佳影響，預期新加坡進口食品、商品及服務成本將持續上升，惟國內生產

創兩年新高，主要得益於電子產品（年增 59.9%），尤其是半導體（73.5%）及黃

## Document 076: 2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026 年 10 月30 日

1/3 
2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測（1 次速報） 
～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

小幅ながら４四半期連続のプラス成長を予想、景気回復基調は維持 
11 月16 日に公表される2026 年７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率を前期比年率＋0.4％（前期比＋
0.1％）と予測する。４四半期連続のプラス成長になるだろう1。 
物価高による下押しや耐久財需要の反動に加え、天候不順の影響もあって個人消費が減少する
一方、設備投資が高い伸びになることが下支えになる見込みである。設備投資の伸びには特殊要
因も一部含まれているが、それを除いても堅調だったと考えられる。個人消費が弱含むなかでも
プラス成長を維持するとみられることは、企業部門の底堅さが景気を下支えしていることを示し
ている。過去３四半期に比較的高い成長が続いた後であることも踏まえれば、今回の減速をもっ
て景気の基調が弱まったと判断する必要はないだろう。家計部門の弱さは懸念材料だが、企業部
門の底堅さは維持されており、景気は引き続き緩やかな回復基調にあると評価できる。

輸入増による成長率押し下げと公的在庫のプラス寄与は表裏一体 
７-９月期のＧＤＰを評価する際には、原油輸入と公的在庫の動きをセットで捉える必要があ
る。４-６月期は、中東情勢の悪化によって原油輸入が激減したことが、ＧＤＰ統計上は輸入減を
通じて成長率を押し上げた。一方、輸入の減少による国内供給の不足を補うために国家備蓄原油
が放出され、公的在庫の取り崩しが進んだ。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.5％Pt
（前期比年率寄与度▲2.1％Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し下げた。輸入減による成長率の押し上げ
は、公的在庫の減少と表裏一体の関係にあった。 
７-９月期は、これと逆の動きが生じるとみられる。政府・民間による代替調達の進展によって
原油輸入が回復するため、統計上は輸入増が成長率の押し下げ要因になる。一方、輸入の回復に
伴って国家備蓄原油の取り崩しは一服しており、公的在庫の減少幅は前期に比べて大きく縮小す
るとみられる。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.4％Pt（前期比年率寄与度＋1.5％
Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し上げることが予想される。

1 10 月 30 日時点で入手可能な経済指標を元に作成している。国際収支や家計調査等、今後公表される経済指標の結果
を踏まえて予測値を修正する可能性がある。

### Page 2

2/3 
輸入増による成長率の押し下げと公的在庫変動による押し上げは、ともに原油の供給環境が改
善したことに伴う動きである。両者を個別に景気の強弱として評価することは適切ではなく、一
体として見るべきだろう。 
むしろ重要なのは、代替調達の進展によって国家備蓄への依存を減らしながら必要な原油を確
保できる状況になり、資源不足が生産活動を大きく制約するリスクが低下していることである。
ＧＤＰ統計上、輸入の増加は成長率の押し下げ要因となるものの、経済実態としては国内で利用
可能な資源の供給回復を意味している。この点は、７-９月期の日本経済を評価するうえで、むし
ろ前向きに捉えるべきだろう。

需要項目別の動向 
個人消費は前期比▲0.2％と小幅減少を予想する。８～９月に各地で相次いだ大雨や台風などの
天候不順が外出機会やサービス消費を抑制した可能性がある。また、４-６月期には自動車やエア
コンなどの耐久財販売が増加したが、７-９月期にはこうした一時的な押し上げ効果が剥落した。
加えて、物価高による節約志向が根強いなか、食料品や日用品などの非耐久財消費にも弱さがみ
られる。高い賃上げや底堅い雇用環境は下支え要因となっているものの、天候不順という一時的
な要因もあって個人消費は総じて力強さを欠く結果になったとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と、高い伸びを予想する。４-６月期には特許権の海外譲渡に伴う研
究開発投資の減少という特殊要因があったが、７-９月期はその影響が剥落することが押し上げ要
因になった面はある。もっとも、こうした特殊要因を除いても設備投資は明確に増加したと考え
られ、底堅い動きとなっている。企業収益が高水準を維持するなか、人手不足に対応した省力化
投資やデジタル・ＡＩ関連投資への需要は根強く、企業の設備投資意欲は引き続き強い。設備投
資計画も高い伸びとなっており、設備投資は均してみれば緩やかな増加基調にあると判断してよ
いだろう。 
なお、特許権の海外への譲渡により、テクニカルに４-６月期はサービス輸出が上振れ、設備投
資が下振れたが、７-９月期はその剥落によりサービス輸出が下振れ、設備投資が上振れとなる見
込みである。両者は相殺し合うため、成長率全体でみれば概ねニュートラルとなる。 
輸出は前期比＋1.2％と４四半期連続の増加を予想する。４-６月期に大口要因によって研究開
発サービスの受取が急増した分が剥落することで、サービス輸出が減少する一方、財輸出が高い
伸びとなり、財貨・サービス輸出全体では増加を維持したとみられる。半導体製造装置などの資
本財輸出が好調だった。地域別では、ＥＵ向けが低調だった一方、ＡＳＥＡＮ、ＮＩＥｓ向けの
資本財輸出が伸びている。また、４-６月期に急減した中東向け輸出が持ち直したことも押し上げ
につながっている。 
輸入は前期比＋3.3％と大幅な増加が予想される。４-６月期には資源輸入の激減により輸入が
大幅に減少していたが、代替調達の進展により７-９月期は明確に持ち直した。この結果、外需寄
与度は前期比▲0.3％Pt（前期比年率▲1.3％Pt）になったと予想される。 
ただし、この外需寄与度のマイナスは、資源輸入の回復に伴う輸入の増加によって生じるもの

である。その裏側では公的在庫変動が前期比＋0.4％ポイント、前期比年率では＋1.5％ポイント
の大幅プラス寄与になると見込まれる。両者は併せて評価する必要がある。 
民間在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.1％Ptと予想する。製品在庫はプラスが予想される一方、流通
在庫の下押しが大きくなるとみられる。 
住宅投資は前期比▲0.9％と、２四半期連続の減少を予想する。建設コストの上昇が下押し要因

Zhen Ding Technology
Valuation
We set a DCF-based TP of NT$750. We use a DCF-based valuation to reflect ZDT's ability to generate stable cash flows. Key
assumptions of our DCF model include a risk-free rate of 5.2% (10-year government bond rate), a market risk premium of 7%,
and an equity beta of 0.89 (1-year weekly share price), deriving a WACC of 10.7%. Our model assumes high-single-digit long-

term growth after 2028E and a 21.6% EBIT margin from 2028E with a gradual annual increase. Our TP is equivalent to 27x/17x
2027E/28E EPS and 4.5x/3.8x 2027E/28E BVPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could cause the stock to trade below our target price include: 1) lower-than-expected
iPhone/Mac/iPad end-demand; 2) lower content value for new smartphone models or pricing pressure; 3) ZDT losing market
share from its FPC/HDI peers; 4) a slower demand in the substrate industry; 5) failure to pass the qualifications of major AI

GPU or ASIC customers.

principal trading revenues. One factor in equity research analyst compensation is arranging corporate access events between
institutional clients and the management teams of covered companies. Typically, company management is more likely to

participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

solely for the recipient and may not be further distributed by the recipient to any third party.

## Document 152: 261111_daiwa_wiwynn

Source PDF: all report pdf/261111_daiwa_wiwynn.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We update our view after 3Q26 results (issued 6 November).

What’s the impact: Fundamentals largely on track. Rising CPU demand 
from agentic AI has lifted our 2026 regular server growth target to >50% 
YoY (vs. 20-30% YoY), making regular servers the key growth driver for 
Wiwynn and helping it offset the slower-than-expected AMZN ASIC AI 
server ramp in 3Q26 (ramping from September vs. original July estimate). 
Looking ahead, we expect the T3 lifecycle to likely peak in 2Q/3Q27, but 
the rising contribution from both Nvidia and AMD GPU AI servers should 
offset the slowdown in 2H27. As for the margin trend, we expect regular 
servers to be at 10% and ASIC AI servers to be at 8%. However, we expect 
the rising GPU AI server to gradually dilute the blended gross margin as 
Wiwynn focuses only on L11, while GPU motherboard (L6) and tray level 
(L10) will be handled by its parent company Wistron (3231 TT, TWD171, 
Buy [1]). As such, we expect the gross margin for Wiwynn’s GPU AI servers 
to be 2-3%, lower than the 4-5% AI of other ODM peers.

3Q26 results beat expectations. Wiwynn reported 3Q26 net income of 
TWD23.36bn (EPS: TWD41.46), beating our expectation by 8%. 3Q26 
sales (TWD434.62bn; up 56% QoQ and 63% YoY) beat our estimate by 6% 
and the Bloomberg consensus estimate by 7% on stronger-than-expected 
regular server shipments. Regular servers accounted for 50-55% of its 
revenue, down from 60-70% in 2Q26, while AI servers contributed 45-50%. 
Gross margin declined by 0.4pp QoQ to 8.9%, but still exceeded our 8.2% 
estimate. Management attributed the sequential margin contraction mainly 
to lower NRE recognition and rising material costs, as not all memory 
components used in regular servers are procured under consignment 
arrangements. However, we believe lower regular server contribution to the 
product mix was key reason. With better operating leverage, operating 
margin improved by 0.2pp QoQ to 7.5%. That said, net profit was dragged 
down by higher interest expense (reaching TWD3bn in 3Q26 vs. TWD2bn 
in 2Q26) and a higher tax rate of 23.2% (vs. 21.1% in 2Q26).

What we recommend: We lift our 2026-27E EPS by 1-5% to reflect 
stronger regular server demand but cut 2028E EPS by 3% to reflect higher 
interest expense assumption. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating but trim our 
12M TP to TWD2,500 (from TWD2,715), based on a target PER of 13x 
(prior: 18x; within its past-2-year trading range of 9-21x) applied to our 1-
year forward diluted EPS, given weaker market sentiment. Key downside 
risks: weaker-than-expected server demand and CSP capex.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 3% above street estimates due to our 
higher regular and AI server shipment assumptions.

Company profile 
Established in 2012 and listed in 2019, Wiwynn (a subsidiary of Wistron Corp) is a dedicated ODM-
direct supplier of servers for hyperscale datacentres, with major customers including Facebook, 
Microsoft, and Amazon. Aside from hyperscaler customers, the company is expanding its target markets 
to what it terms "Next Wave" customers, including tier-2 cloud service providers and telecom operators, 
which could bring growth opportunities related to edge computing and open RAN.

### Page 5

5

Wiwynn Corp (6669 TT): 11 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 11 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Wiwynn plans to have seven to nine directors on its board who will serve three-year terms in
accordance with the Articles of Incorporation in Taiwan. Currently, Wiwynn has 9 board
members with five independent directors. The background of independent directors is diversified
and their competence includes technology, business management, and law. We deem the
diversified background as a positive factor for the company’s board quality. Wiwynn held 9 board
meetings in 2025 and the average attendance rate was around 99%.
The Chairman does not serve concurrently as the President or CEO, which we think is a positive
for board and executive management as well as external shareholder interest.
Capital management 2
Wiwynn declared cash DPS of TWD145 and a stock dividend of TWD20 per share for 2025,
representing a total payout ratio of 60.0%. We note that investors favour its high dividend payout
ratio policy. However, Wiwynn has been expanding its own manufacturing capacity in Malaysia,
Tainan, Mexico and the US, making its business model less asset-light than before. With the
server market still in the high growth period and the payout ratio remaining at around 60%, we
expect the favourable dividend policy to be sustained going forward.
Related party &
transaction 3
In 2025, revenue from related parties accounted for 0.2% of total revenue, while purchases from
related parties were 9.2% of cost of sales. The main purchase counterparty is Wistron (3231 TT;
Wiwynn's parent company), with purchases from Wistron accounting for 8.09% of total
procurement. While Wiwynn is a professional assembly vendor for data center servers, it still
outsources a portion of its production capacity to its parent company, Wistron. At the same time,
Wiwynn has expanded its own production bases in Malaysia, Tainan, Mexico and the US,
reducing its reliance on Wistron. This business model still causes meaningful transactions with
related parties, although the exposure has declined materially. Indeed, we are concerned
whether Wistron collects fair manufacturing fees from Wiwynn. As the company states that
trading terms of purchase transactions with related parties cannot be compared with third-party
vendors due to product specifications, we are not able to assess this concern.
S Supply chain management 1
Wiwynn focuses on providing various products and system solutions for hyperscale datacentres
and cloud infrastructures. There are over 100 suppliers providing key components, electronic
materials, and mechanical materials in Wiwynn’s supply chain. For the new joiners that target to
penetrate into Wiwynn’s supply chain, Wiwynn has formed the “Supplier Evaluation Group” to
conduct: 1) supplier management and supply capabilities evaluation, 2) technical capability
development assessment, and 3) partner quality assurance (these include mainly topics
undertaken by RBA, such as no child labour, no conflict minerals, no use of environmentally
hazardous substances, etc.). New suppliers are also required to be ISO9001, ISO14001, IECQ
QC 080000, and OHSAS 18001 (ISO 45001) qualified.
Wiwynn has strong supply chain risk management by conducting audits regularly. Apart from
considering the technical skills, quality date, delivery dates and price completeness of suppliers,
Wiwynn audits its suppliers to endeavour to protect the environment, ensure safety and health of
the environment, and emphasise the importance of human rights, based on Wiwynn’s CSR
Management System.
S Data security 2
To serve world-class cloud service providers, Wiwynn has been constantly improving its
information security management. Employees are educated on the protection of the product,
technology, and information privacy of customers.
In response to the global trends in data protection laws that tend to strengthen the security of
classified information, Wiwynn has mandated that all assigned PCs have the Windows Bitlocker
HDD encryption activated to avoid classified data being accessed without permission. In 2020, it
also introduced Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA). Beyond the security measures for hardware,
the company controls access to office, labs, and conference room in a robust manner to prevent
theft of the hardware.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20 개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500 억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다. 
투자판단 3 단계(6 개월기준) 15%이상 → 매수 / -15%~15% → 중립 / -15%미만 → 매도

## Document 241: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸3Q26 Review_ 악재 소멸 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
악조건 속에서도 이 정도 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 영업실적은 매출액 5조 7,536억원(yoy +30.2%, qoq 
`-9.1%), 영업이익 8,992억원(yoy +61.3%, qoq -13.5%)으로 당사 추정치에 대체로 
부합하였다. 조업일수 감소와 원화 강세로 매출액이 감소했으며, 임단협 타결로 
인한 성과금 및 격려금이 당분기에 비용으로 반영되었다. 해양플랜트 부문에서는 
RUYA 프로젝트의 공사 진행률 확대와 TRION 프로젝트의 C/O(계약 변경) 
180억원이 추가로 반영되며 매출액이 전분기대비 0.7% 증가하였다. 엔진기계 
부문은 환율 하락을 판가 상승과 D/F(이중연료) 비중 증가로 상쇄하며 전분기와 
유사한 영업실적을 달성했다. 
2027년에도 지속될 수주 시점 효과 
3분기 대형선 야드의 수주 시점별 매출 비중은 2022년 4%, 2023년 45%, 2024년 
31%, 2025년 20% 수준으로 파악된다. 상대적으로 선가가 낮았던 2022~2023년 
물량이 여전히 매출의 절반 가량을 차지하고 있는 셈이다. 따라서 2027년 말까지도 
고선가 물량의 매출 비중 확대가 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이는 LNG운반선 매출 
비중 감소에도 불구하고 수익성을 방어할 수 있는 핵심 요인으로 작용할 전망이다.   
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 550,000원으로 하향 
투자의견은 매수로 유지하나, 목표주가는 550,000원으로 34.5% 하향한다. 환율 
가정을 기존 1,450원 수준에서 1,400원으로 조정함에 따라 목표주가 산정의 기준이 
되는 2027~2028년 실적추정치를 하향조정하였다. 또한 미국 네오 클라우드 
업체들의 자금 조달 여건 악화로 데이터센터 증설이 지연되고 있어, 발전용 엔진 
증설을 근거로 동사에 부여하였던 프리미엄을 제거하여 P/E 배수를 19.8배로 
하향조정했다. 그러나 본업인 상선 부문은 향후 고부가가치 가스선의 발주 강세를 
전망하기 때문에 투자의견은 매수로 유지한다.

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports.1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

4 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.
Exhibit 3: Europe's Upcoming Elections Represent Around Half of EU GDP and Population.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

and interest rate outlook, rising
competition and slowing demand
from China continue to challenge
key segments of the Consumer
Discretionary sector. And limited
direct AI concentration also leaves
Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and
emerging Asia.

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026,2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.

for investors seeking income and can

rate volatility. We expect demand for
U.S. assets to remain healthy and favor
the U.S. over developed economies in

fiscal instability.

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

## Document 295: Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-15)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-15).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-15
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

2027年大幅增加晶片供應，以因應AI產品需
求快速成長，執行長蘇姿丰更赴台與供應鏈
夥伴討論擴充CPU與GPU產能，顯示AI半導
體長期發展趨勢強勁，激勵科技相關類股表
現。此外，G7釋放緊急原油與柴油庫存後，
減輕市場對能源價格與通膨的部分擔憂，亦
帶動非必需消費產業上漲。

► 美國9月非農數據公布後一度令10年期公債
殖利率出現明顯回落。然觀察最新ISM服務
業之價 格分 項指 數 較上月 出現反彈， 並創
2022年7月以來最高水準，反映運輸及原物
料成本上升，顯示通膨壓力仍可能持續向其
他產業傳導，公債殖利率因而再度反彈並持

擔憂持續走貶可能再度引發干預，令短線日
圓呈現區間震盪走勢。
► G7宣布透過國際能源總署協調釋放1億桶柴
油及原油等緊急儲備，令油價短線回落，然
隨中東衝突前景未明，供應不穩之變數短期
難解，多空因素交錯恐使油價於高檔進行反
覆波動。

► 美國經濟仍具韌性，惟通膨壓力猶存。觀察
美國9月ISM服務業指數表現，內需活動維持
穩健，企業投入成本壓力亦有所升溫；另一
方面，受地緣政治因素影響，美國柴油價格
大幅攀升，而柴油為卡車運輸、農業及製造
業的重要燃料，若價格持續維持高檔，恐進
一步增添通膨壓力。考量聯準會偏鷹立場，
且當前通膨降溫進程不如預期，年底前仍保
有升息空間。
► 美國債務總額突破40兆美元，預估2026年公
眾持有聯邦債務占GDP的比率將達101%，
財政可持續性風險升溫。與此同時，AI基礎

建設舉債熱潮，疊加中東局勢動盪帶來的通
膨不確定性，迫使投資人重新定價長天期債
券風險，要求更高的風險溢酬。隨實質殖利
率上行，美債10年期殖利率揚升並持續高懸
於5%水準之上，30年期長債殖利率亦同步站
上近20年來高點。隨長天期債券利率續揚，
殖利率曲線趨陡峭，債券配置宜先避開長天
期部位，聚焦中短天期債券鎖利機會。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 距離美國期中選舉投票僅剩不到1個月，觀察
Polymarket賭盤預估數據顯示，眾議院選情
較為明朗，民主黨有超過9成的機率控制眾議
院。但參議院選情則尚有變數，今年以來兩
黨均互有領先，雖然數據顯示民主黨掌控參
議院機率呈現上揚，然隨時序進入選前最後
衝刺期，參議院能否亦由民主黨掌控仍待觀
察。故就選前而言，由於不同政黨的政策傾
向差異，潛在受惠產業亦有不同，選舉結果
的不確定性恐令投資人不易大舉押注股市。
► 另一方面，油價高檔徘迴，導致年底前通膨
擔憂仍存，加上AI企業發債需求成長、美國
財政體質惡化等因素共同發酵，推動近期美
債殖利率逐步攀高，並令股市預期本益比壓
力延續，進而限縮了投資人短期對美股的追
價動能，短期指數恐維持震盪輪動格局。可
留意抗波動、體質佳且具價值優勢之標的，
待期中選舉結束與3Q26財報展開後，不確定
性減退將有助資金回歸長線AI題材。
殖利率攀升令美股本益比面臨壓力參議院期中選舉選情仍具變數
期中選前擾動與殖利率走高，美股短期恐仍震盪輪動

### Page 7

7
投資焦點日股
► 9月日本銀行決議升息1碼，明確表示潛在
CPI已接近2%，未來也將繼續調高利率，政
策重點開始轉向防範通膨向上偏離目標。然
因有2位委員反對升息，加上總裁植田和男會
後談話偏向中性，令市場解讀此次升息未如
預期鷹派，美日利差不易快速縮窄，故9月會
議後日元反而呈現利多出盡的回貶反應。
► 觀察今年以來日股面臨短線日圓升值的狀態
並未出現顯著走弱態勢。一方面反映企業治
理改革推動股東報酬提升已成為支撐股價的
重要結構性因素。另一方面，升息代表日本
經濟已具備一定的健康程度，貨幣政策正常
化將不致對需求帶來衝擊。因此，隨BoJ延
續升息方向，將擴大銀行淨利差，改善其獲
利結構，並提升金融資產收益。儘管近期整
體日股預期獲利成長走勢略顯停滯，然日本
銀行股在總 體經 濟 與貨幣 政策環 境的 支持
下，獲利成長有望逆勢攀升，料將成為後續
資金關注重點。
日圓升值對日股影響似乎有限，關注銀行股獲利走強BoJ升息方向延續，惟其步伐仍取決於經濟與物價
BoJ政策保持正常化路線，日本銀行股有望持續受惠

### Page 8

8
股市
 公債殖利率走高雖可能壓抑股債市場近期動能，然而觀察美股第三季獲利年增率有望仍為雙位數成
長，或支撐股市表現。市場持續關注AI資本支出是否帶來獲利成長，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場
動盪，可分散配置於不同國家與產業，降低市場波動影響。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評
價面壓力等紛擾帶來干擾，可布局防禦股增強抗震力。長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且
基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融及醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。殖利率偏高環境建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率走
升時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸

引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
 短期受美國雙率走高有壓，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 387: Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

Threats to European diesel imports: risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased 
risk of price rises

In Europe, the possibility of a US ban on diesel exports has heightened concerns about price trends for this widely used 
fuel1, which has already risen significantly since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran. It has become a major political issue 
on both sides of the Atlantic in the run-up to decisive elections.

A particularly tight diesel market due to a shortage of buffer stocks 
Following the outbreak of the conflict, diesel prices did indeed see some of the sharpest rises on the oil market due to particularly 
tight market conditions. Since the end of February, whilst the price of a barrel of crude has risen by 46%, diesel prices have soared 
by 100% in Europe and by 76% in the United States.

The factors alleviating pressure on the refined products market, and more specifically the diesel market, were more limited than in 
the case of crude oil. Shipments passing through the Strait of Hormuz (around 17% of maritime diesel trade prior to the conflict) 
have fallen by around 75%. Added to this is the halt in Russian exports due to war damage, which is depriving the diesel market of 
around 12% of its maritime exports. Furthermore, unlike with crude oil, China has not acted as a stabilising force in the diesel 
market. On the contrary, the energy crisis has led to a halt in Chinese exports of refined products (including diesel) to the rest of 
Asia from April onwards. The resumption of Chinese exports over the last two months remains fragile, given, in particular, that 
diesel stocks are at their lowest level in 15 months.

The United States has eased these tensions in the diesel market by increasing its exports of refined products (up 25% on average 
in the first half of 2026 compared with the average over the last five years). This rise in exports has required high refinery utilisation 
rate (close to maximum capacity) and, to a lesser extent, a reduction in stocks (-11% compared with the average over the last five 
years as at mid-September for middle distillates2). All these factors have contributed to tightening the US domestic market and 
pushed retail diesel prices to record levels. Road diesel prices (US average) have risen by 68% since the end of February and by 
74% year-on-year.

A structurally high level of vulnerability in Europe… 
Against this market backdrop, characterised by mounting tensions and very limited room for manoeuvre, Europe would be 
particularly hard hit by a reduction in – or even a complete halt to – US exports. According to Eurostat, imports accounted for 46% 
of European demand for diesel in the first half of 2026, with 40% of these imports coming from the United States. Europe, which

1 Diesel accounts for 17% of the primary energy mix in the EU and 7.3% in the United States. 
2 A category of refined petroleum products comprising mainly diesel and aviation fuel.

### Page 2

has lost a great deal of production capacity in recent years, is operating its refineries at near-maximum capacity. Its room for 
manoeuvre is therefore limited.

… But the risk of a shortage has been ruled out in the short term 
The risk of a shortage, however, appears limited in the short term. The situation regarding European stocks is not comfortable, but 
it is not alarming. Commercial diesel stocks in the ARA region3 are currently at historically low levels (around 12 million barrels), 
23% below their level at the end of September 2025. As for emergency stocks (EU Emergency Stocks), these are, according to the 
European Commission, at a sufficient level. Eurostat estimates this figure at 270 million barrels in June 2026 (stable from a year 
earlier), representing approximately 60 days’ worth of EU consumption. Europe imports around 0.9 mb/d of diesel from the United 
States, and emergency stocks would therefore cover around 60 days’ consumption at 4.5 mb/d.

The rise in prices could be significant 
Whatever the outcome of the conflict, diesel prices are likely to continue to rise in the short term. According to our base-case 
scenario, crude oil prices are expected to remain very high in Q4 2026 due to ongoing tensions in the Persian Gulf, whilst a return 
to normal levels of diesel exports from Russia and the Middle East appears unlikely by that time.

Among the factors likely to cause future tensions, the start of the maintenance season in Europe could affect production levels, 
whilst demand for diesel in the United States is typically high in the autumn (agricultural harvests and pre-winter storage). In a 
scenario where tensions remain low, the diesel refining margin (crack spread) could rise by 5% in Q4 compared with its level at the 
end of September 2026, before gradually falling back during 2027. An intermediate scenario (a 10% rise in the crack spread in Q4 
2026) would result in moderate constraints on US diesel exports and a drawdown of European stocks. Finally, an adverse scenario 
(a 35% rise in the crack spread in Q4 2026) would imply severe restrictions on US diesel exports and a delayed European response.

What impact will this have on inflation in the euro area? 
According to our estimates, the rise in diesel prices would exacerbate the expected peak in inflation in the euro area without altering 
its timing. In our baseline scenario for September 2026 (see our recently published EcoPerspective), headline inflation would peak 
at 4.1% y/y in January 2027 before gradually falling back towards 2% by the end of the year. The two least adverse scenarios 
would add only a limited amount to the peak: +0.1 pp in the first scenario (+5% for the crack spread in Q4 compared with the end 
of Q3) and +0.2 pp in the intermediate scenario (+10%), with inflation reaching 4.2% and 4.3% respectively in January 2027. 
However, there is an upside risk in the second scenario linked to the uncertain extent of the spillover effects. In both cases, the 
main impact would be felt through fuel prices (which quickly reflect rising market prices) and would fade over the course of 2027. 
In the case of the adverse scenario (+35%), the additional inflation would be close to 0.6 pp by early 2027, pushing inflation above 
4.6%. A shock of this magnitude would, in fact, reduce businesses’ ability to absorb rising costs, which would lead to price increases 
across a broader range of goods (food, industrial goods). Subsequently, the effect would only fade gradually.

Pascal Devaux 
Economist, Middle East, Western Balkans – Energy 
pascal.devaux@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe, Eurozone, International Trade 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

3 Amsterdam-Rotterdam-Antwerp, Europe’s main hub for the storage of raw materials.

重點摘要
• 美國多州出現暫停資料中心建
設的政策訴求，資金可能加速
流向規則較為明確的州。
• 電力股的投資邏輯正在轉向，
未來將更看重獲利品質與監管
可預測性。
• AI資料中心電力需求仍是公用
事業3Q26財報重點，但焦點轉
向如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成
本。
《部分電力股評價將更看重監管可預測性》
在2026年美國期中選舉涉及的36場州長選戰中，已
有20州出現暫停資料中心建設或許可的政策訴求。
電價、用水與基礎設施成本的分攤爭議，正改寫各
州爭取AI投資的方向，資金可能從監管混沌的地區，
加速流向規則較為明確的州。德州為這輪監管分化
中最值得關注、也最棘手的地區。德州電網接入申
請佇列已成長到約474吉瓦，其中約九成來自資料

中心，而德州州長已宣布暫停核准新的資料中心接
入州內電網，9月底環境委員會的暫停令進一步擴及
自備電源的離網專案，意味企業即使自己發電，也
未必能繞過許可關卡。
相較之下，在大規模用電需求湧入前，先把監管制
度架好的州或許更具產業前景。懷俄明、明尼蘇達、
愛荷華、威斯康辛、內華達、南卡羅來納與田納西
州為潛在的資金承接者。明尼蘇達已立法防止大客
戶把成本轉嫁給一般用戶，科羅拉多的大負荷費率
方案則要求至少15年合約、80%最低計費需求與提
前退出費，把部分退出風險交還給客戶。電力股的
投資邏輯正在轉向，未來部分公用事業股的評價分
化，將從過去單純看需求規模，轉向更看重獲利品
質與監管可預測性。
《AI資料中心電力需求仍為近期財報重點，但焦點
轉向如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成本》
隨著3Q26財報陸續披露，美國公用事業公司對AI資
料中心用電需求普遍釋出極為樂觀且迫切的長線成
長展望。以FirstEnergy為例，受惠於毗鄰北弗吉尼
亞數據中心走廊的優勢，西弗吉尼亞州等地的資料
中 心 相 關 負 載 需 求 激 增 137% 達到4.3 吉瓦。

American Electric Power其累積的增量負載承諾已
飆升至56吉瓦，半年內暴增一倍。但公用事業高層
的態度在積極擴產中也帶有審慎風險控管，為了避
免新建發電廠及電網升級的費用侵蝕一般用戶權益，
業者正重新設計合約，要求科技公司簽署最低電費
與信用擔保條款。整體而言，公用事業財報反映出
資料中心用電需求並未降溫，但產業焦點已從「如
何接到訂單」轉向「如何平衡供應瓶頸與監管成本」
的階段。
2

➢ 美國國會變天：民主黨不僅重新奪回眾議院，也
以遠超預期的幅度拿下參議院多數席次。整體政
策方向其實不會發生重大改變，但民主黨未來可
能利用財政法案與債務上限談判，推動限制對伊
朗戰爭、部分撤銷關稅以及影響財政支出。

建設是否受到影響，是目前市場最關注的焦點。
本行認為短期逆風確實存在，但不影響長期建置
趨勢。

美國國會變天，AI概念受挫然未見風險情緒，殖利率回落
AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊
盤勢回顧
美10年期利率回到5%以下，信用利差自高點收斂
3
• AI概念受挫，資金輪動至其他板塊：民主黨全面掌控國會後，市場擔憂AI產業、大型雲端服務與資料中心的監
管與審查將顯著收緊，使相關類股、日台韓股市均下挫，不過資金輪動到其他產業，支撐美股指數維持穩定。
• 財政紛擾仍存但支出展望中性，美10年債利率來到5%以下：市場開始反應財政政策可望趨於中性，再加上地
緣政治有望緩和，布蘭特原油跌破100美元/桶，支持美國公債利率持續走低至5%以下，自10月高點以來下滑
近50個基點。殖利率下滑輔以金融市場不確定因素降低支撐股市反彈，同步帶動美國信用債利差收斂，11月以
來美國信用債總報酬緩步走揚。

©  CTBC
兩黨對立將放大財政期限影響與紛擾，整體財政支出趨於中性
兩黨對立，財政期限的重要性上升
政策影響
2027年的財政動能預計將大致維持中性
4
• 財政期限的重要性上升，帶來政治紛擾：民主黨可利用具強制性的時間節點，作為政策讓步的槓桿。第一個要
注意的時間點是12/11政府支出授權到期，不過將期限延到2027年2至3月的機率較高(否則政府可能關門)。另
外推論1Q27將臨近債務上限，以目前的財政部存款和非常規措施的使用，最多可支應至2027年8月至10月。
• 財政政策整體趨於中性：分裂政府通常意味著較不具擴張性的財政政策，而眾議院掌握聯邦預算的撥款權，將
可能透過拒絕追加或直接限制戰爭預算的方式來迫使戰事降溫。然民主黨同時也可能與共和黨協議，以撤銷去
年大而美法案的支出削減措施、恢復健康保險補貼等作為交換條件，讓白宮得以增加國防預算。綜合來看，短
線面臨預算協調的紛擾，美債殖利率波動仍可能偏高，但趨勢上政策制衡效果增強，有利減輕殖利率的壓力。
資料來源：左， 高盛，2026/11，右，高盛，2026/11。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11

關稅結構可能改變，資料中心監管程度提高但不影響建置趨勢
民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅，但整體稅率變化有限
政策影響
對資料中心監管程度將提高，但非禁止建置
5
• 民主黨將嘗試撤回部分關稅，但整體稅率變化可能有限：民主黨領袖已表示將嘗試撤銷已實施的部分關稅，但
川普政府仍可利用301條款推動新的關稅，整體有效關稅稅率變化可能有限，但其中可能的重點是降低對加拿大
的關稅。
• 對資料中心監管程度將提高，不影響建置趨勢：傳統共和黨票倉的德州變天，而德州是最大的資料中心建設地
區，新任的民主黨州長可能要求企業自行承擔電力及電網擴建成本、保障居民用水優先權，並在保護規範明確
之前影響資料中心的建設進度。不過本行認為，除了要求企業揭露資料來源與訓練資訊、建立安全治理架構，
以及落實其他風險管理措施等規定之外，尚無太多州級監管制度被認為具有高度限制性。而在聯邦層級，大規
模的新監管措施即使被國會提出，也很可能被川普否決，且AI競爭力仍是跨黨派國策，建設趨勢將持續推進。
資料來源：左， 高盛 ，2026/10，右，高盛，2026/10。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

### Page 6

©  CTBC
AI 資料中心已從「產業政策」轉變為「民生電價議題」
超大型業者算力供給轉吃緊
產業影響
2030年電力缺口收斂
6
• 德州資料中心暫停令，AI算力布局的轉變與供需影響：德州8月初頒布的暫停令，同步凍結了新資料中心的興建
（僅限未動工新案）與電力輸送。在扣除被淘汰的虛報專案需求後，預估部分專案將外溢至其他電力更吃緊的州，
導致全美電力缺口淨擴大約 3–6 GW。若Hinojosa 就任後延續暫停令執行超過一年，其影響2028 年全美電力缺
口將進一步擴大，2030年供需缺口預估將逐步收斂。另一方面，暫停令讓2028年本就吃緊的算力市場更緊，維
持算力租金的高檔區間，對超大型業者而言建置成本增加，與手上既有的算力可以提高售價，兩者相抵，2028
年企業獲利的預估影響僅在1-3%水準。
• 提高監管是正在發生的實質逆風，喊停是低機率的極端政治口號：AI 技術攸關美國的核心基本國力。民主黨的
政策向來主張「調整與監管」，而非全面「廢止」。因此，未來更嚴格的許可審查與補助限制，勢必會提高雲

端大廠在德州擴張的時間成本與資本風險。然而，也可能推動針對資料中心的特定稅種，例如聯邦總收入稅，
或針對資料中心電力消耗的消費稅。民主黨也有可能在新會期提出比共和黨更強制性更嚴格監管的版本。民主
黨掌控國會後，最可能成法的是「電費成本分攤」立法，因為川普已將此議題列為自己的政績。
資料來源：左，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11，右，blackrock，2026/4。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/4

©  CTBC
健護最具兩黨協商的價值，醫療產業相對受惠
12/11為醫療補貼可以放進談判的時間點
產業影響
健護未來一年的本益比仍合理
7
• ACA補貼及醫療補助是最可能與川普達成交易的領域：民主黨贏得參眾兩院，不等於民主黨執政，兩黨仍須協
商。就健護產業而言，相對有利影響的領域在2025年底已到期的<平價醫療法案(ACA)>增強型保費補貼，受淨保
費上升，已有超過200萬人放棄投保，及受<大而美法案>被削減的醫療補助(Medicaid)在2025~2034年超過
9,000億美元。現ACA進行增強型補貼的恢復提案還在進行中，需總統簽署才能立法恢復，可以是談判交換條件
的一部分；Medicaid刪減是已成法、分階段生效，無法被推翻，只能延緩Medicaid的刪減，其中對工作的要求
限制於今年底生效，早於新國會就任，12/11臨時撥款法案談判是可以納入談判作為局部修正空間的時間點。
• 健護是民主黨國會最直接的受惠產業，健護未來一年本益比仍相對合理：預期若醫療補助刪減延後，醫院將受
惠，ACA補貼若部分恢復，將有利ACA型管理式醫療業者的投保人數止跌回升。以健護未來一年本益比來看，

目前股價仍相對合理。

資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢
期中選舉前股市較為承壓，但無礙趨勢
市場觀點
資料來源：(左) 高盛，2026/11；(右) 摩根大通，2026/11 。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11
分裂國會在兩年任期內的表現優於單一政黨執政
8
1974年以來標普500指數期中選舉前後的走勢
• 期中選舉前股市較為承壓，但無礙趨勢：根據歷史經驗，期中選舉年通常是總統任期中回撤較深的一年，且選
前的股市波動會明顯放大，然在選後不確定性消退，市場表現往往有所改善，標普500指數在選後的3個月，歷
史中位數報酬率為+6%，而分裂政府(總統和國會分屬不同黨派)的歷史報酬率通常較佳。
• AI相關類股波動高，資金輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢：真正驅動市場的因素仍是利率、地緣、
AI，而不是單純從歷史規律推演，近期市場擔憂雲端業者的現金流和債務問題，因此若資料中心的建置時程受
到影響，將進一步影響相關業者的營收和償債時程，提高AI相關類股的波動；另外眾議院是否對川普提出彈劾，

也將造成政治上的波動。但就整體趨勢來說，既有政策不會因此出現重大轉折，穩健成長的企業獲利提供重要
支撐，AI類股修正時期資金仍在其他類股間輪動而未明顯離場，美股短線有壓然無礙趨勢。

©  CTBC
短線待消化北亞出口疑慮，長線行情回歸基本面
情緒回穩後聚焦基本面與地緣動盪 短線消化資料中心建設監管趨嚴對北亞出口影響，
然長線美核心政策延續性依舊，估計影響有限
9
• 選後反彈高度取決景氣與政策推演：回顧過去美股期中選舉期間，新興市場選前壓抑，選後大多繳出正報酬佳
績，上漲比率超越五成。以參眾兩院控制權皆翻轉的1994年、2006年來看，1944年因美聯準會啟動快速升息，
加上墨西哥金融危機爆發，選後新興市場進入近一年空頭走勢。2006年即便也是分裂政府，全球宏觀環境受惠
貿易繁榮、商品超級周期推動經濟成長，新興市場選後12個月累積報酬亮眼。總體來說，選後不確定性下降，
新興市場可望迎反彈走勢，而即便美國政治版圖翻轉，選後新興市場走勢取決於景氣、政策推演與地緣干擾。
• 市場短線待消化資料中心建設進程對北亞出口影響：美期中選舉對新興市場影響較大面向，主要是關稅與貿易
政策、債務上限，以及對資料中心建設的態度。關稅主導權在白宮推估影響有限 ; 預算與債限談判或對利率造
成波動 ; 短線需消化選後對資料中心監管趨嚴影響北亞出口的失望情緒，然預計長線AI建置仍屬國家重心政策。

資料來源：左， Bloomberg ，2026/11，右， Bloomberg ，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11。

• 期中選舉共和黨大敗，美元走弱：週三市場確認共和黨於期中選舉失利後，美元兌主要貨幣全面回檔。本次期
中選舉由民主黨取得參、眾兩院多數。民主黨掌控參議院後，可阻止或延後川普的人事任命；贏得眾議院後，
則可對政府展開調查，甚至進一步推動彈劾程序。歷史經驗顯示，當民主黨於期中選舉後同時掌控參、眾兩院
時，美元指數平均下跌4.6%，6次中有4次下跌。整體而言，選舉結果短期內可能因預算協商及債務上限僵局

升高政治風險。2027年美國將再度面臨債務上限問題，美伊地緣政治及貿易戰等變數亦將牽動實質利率走勢。
• 2027年債務上限恐成兩黨鬥爭焦點，不利美元：12月11日前，現任國會通過持續撥款決議的可能性偏高，但
美國聯邦政府收支預計將於2027年觸及債務上限。受債務上限約束的債務規模預估於2027年3月接近法定上限
500億美元以內，之後隨4月稅收入帳而暫時下降；但若財政赤字增幅高於預期，X-Day恐提前到來。展望
2027年，美國財政議題仍將面臨逆風。共和黨失去完全執政雖有助抑制財政擴張，但政治僵局恐加劇債務上限、
違約及信評遭調降風險，使美元波動加大。

3Q26 EPS 37.67 元，分別低於元大/市場預期 1~3 成 
3Q26 台幣營收 QoQ+241%、YoY+297%，主要受到 TRN3 大量出貨，整體
N3/N2 的營收占比從 47%跳增至 80%。惟受到量產營收佔比提高、部份專
案 NRE 遞延認列，使得本季毛利率季減 16.8 個百分點達 18%及 OPM 季減
7.6 個百分點達 13.9%，低於公司先前預期 2 個百分點，業外收益 4.53 億元、
EPS 37.67 元/季增 88%，獲利分別於元大/低於市場預期 1~3 成。 
4Q26 營收、獲利將創新高 
展望 4Q26 TRN3 持續大幅放量及新專案 tape out，營收、獲利持續創新高，
其中預期毛利率估計與 3Q26 接近，2026 全年營收估計落在 21~22 億美元、
毛利率>20%。元大預估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+15%、YoY+536%、毛利率估計
18%、推估 EPS 43.17 元/季增 15%，較前次報告下修 15%，雖然上修營收
預估，但下修毛利率假設基礎。 
TRN3/4 推升 26~28 年營收達新高，第二家 CSP 具想像空間 
展望 2027 年預估營收有機會年增三位數，主要成長來自 TRN3 本身出貨倍
增及公司取得更高的量產配置外，2Q27 新增一家美系 CSP 客戶，估計訂單
規模達 10 億美元，細節不透露。下一代 TRN4 製程採 N2 GAA 的 Chiplet
架構、2.5D 封裝，公司負責所有 Die 的 physical design 與整合，包括
compute die 與 I/O die，整體 N2 專案的設計、整合及量產複雜度提高，估
計單價、潛在市場規模顯著超過 N3。中長期 CSP 由傳統晶片統包轉向
COT 分工模式，趨勢對公司有利。最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 118/186 元，
分別下修 15/上修 8%，係因 TRN3 出貨上修及新增 CSP 客戶而上修營收預
估，然毛利率表現低於公司先前指引而下修假設基礎。

元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 EPS 37.67 元，低於預期。展望 4Q26 TRN3 量能放大、新專案
tape out，營收、獲利將創歷史新高，推估 EPS 43.17 元。  
⧫  
展望 2027 年 AWS TRN3 仍為主要成長動能，除了 TRN3 本身出貨倍
增及公司量產配置提高，2Q27 新增第二家 CSP，將帶動營收有機會
年增三位數，TRN4 接續 27~28 年動能更為強勁。 
⧫  
最新預估 2026/27 年 EPS 118/186 元，分別下修 15%/上修 8%，雖然
營收展望樂觀，然毛利率低於預期。維持買進評等、惟 27 年目標本益
比降至 25 倍，推得目標價 4600 元，儘管看好 TRN 3、4 帶動未來
2~3 年成長趨勢不變，然短期評價受系統性風險、籌碼面而有所下修。

資料來源：公司資料、Grand View Research、元大投顧預估 資料來源：元大投顧預估 
TRN3/4 推升 26~28 年營收達新高，第二家 CSP 具想像空間 
展望 2027 年預估營收有機會年增三位數，主要成長來自 TRN3 本身出貨倍增及公司取得更高的量
產配置外，2Q27 新增一家美系 CSP 客戶，估計訂單規模達 10 億美元，細節不透露。下一代
TRN4 製程採 N2 GAA 的 Chiplet 架構、2.5D 封裝，公司負責所有 Die 的 physical design 與整合，
包括 compute die 與 I/O die，整體 N2 專案的設計、整合及量產複雜度提高，估計單價、潛在市場
規模顯著超過 N3。中長期 CSP 由傳統晶片統包轉向 COT 分工模式，趨勢對公司有利。最新預估
2026/27 年 EPS118/186 元，分別下修 15/上修 8%，係因 TRN3 出貨上修及新增 CSP 客戶而上修
營收預估，然毛利率表現低於公司先前指引而下修假設基礎。

Trainium 3 開始量產，推升 26 年營收倍數成長 
AWS 為公司最大客戶，營運表現與 AWS ASIC roadmap 連動性高，新一代 Trainium3 已於 2Q26
量產，受限於晶圓、封測產能吃緊，訂單排程已至 11 月，產品生命週期估計二年，根據供應鏈調
查，Trainium 3 出貨量估計約 200 萬顆，估計 2026 年 AWS 營收佔比達 7 成。下一代 Trainium 4
採台積 N2 製程，並且支援 NV Link Fusion 技術，擴大結合 GPU 生態系，客戶目標 2026 年 tape 
out、27 年底進入 prototype、28 年量產。除了 Trainium 以外，公司已取得多個 N2 設計專案，客
戶包括 HPC、北美 Networking，預期 N2 為下一波成長動能。 
圖 5： AWS AI 晶片 Roadmap

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧預估

Trainium 3 採用的 N3 製程與 HBM3E 高頻寬記憶體，使其在模型參數規模擴張下，提供更符合大
型訓練叢集需求的計算密度。與前代相比，Trainium 3 以更佳的運算效率與能耗比滿足雲端訓練的

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第4 頁，共 19頁  
成本結構要求，使 AI ASIC 在 AWS 內部的部屬比例持續提升。由於 Trainium 3 晶片邏輯面積、電
源網絡設計、時序收斂與封裝整合的難度均大幅高於前代，世芯在後段設計以及與台積電 CoWoS 
先進封裝流程的協同整合中扮演更為關鍵角色，主因後段設計與 DFT（Design-For-Test）複雜度
全面升級，反映製程推進至 N3 世代， ASIC 對高階設計服務供應商的依賴度進一步強化。 
展望 Trainium 4，其採用 N2 製程並首次導入 NVLink Fusion 技術，是 AWS 於 AI 訓練基礎架構上
的策略性變動。NVLink Fusion 使 Trainium 4 能與 NVIDIA GPU、CPU 及其他介面形成異質叢集
（heterogeneous cluster），並在跨晶片間實現高頻寬、低延遲的連結，讓 AWS 能以 ASIC + 
GPU 的混合架構（hybrid architecture）支撐大型模型訓練需求。此設計不僅能降低單純依賴 GPU 
所帶來的成本與供應鏈風險，也為 AWS Data Center 架構引入更高彈性，使 ASIC 不再僅限於推
論或中小型模型訓練，而成為可與 GPU 生態系共同運作的訓練加速器。此外 Trainium 4 的封裝規
格預期將導入更高能耗管理密度、更大晶片尺寸與更多 I/O die 整合，使物理設計與封裝協同需求
顯著增加，將使後段設計服務與 Turnkey 所需承擔的工程案量及技術門檻較 Trainium 3 提升。

圖 6： NVLink Fusion 架構示意

資料來源：NVIDIA Developer 
AWS 公布的架構方向顯示，其未來 AI 加速器的更新週期有望自過往 2.5–3 年縮短至 18–24 個月，
反映 LLM 訓練需求成長速度快於傳統半導體製程演進。AWS 持續提升自研 ASIC 在資料中心中占
比，並以 Trainium 系列與 GPU 共融的方式建立成本更具優勢的訓練叢集，使其 AI Infrastructure 
Roadmap 呈現加速態勢。觀察世芯過去營運成果可發現其營運高度連動於 AWS 專案，本中心預
期 2026 年將受惠於 Trainium 3 全面放量，並在 2027 年起隨 Trainium 4 的推出進入下一波量產高
峰。隨著 ASIC 在大型模型訓練的採用率提升與 AWS 從以 GPU 搭配 ASIC 轉向兩者並行的混合叢
集策略，預期世芯未來 2-3 年營運具備明確的擴張動能與高能見度。

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第5 頁，共 19頁  
產業概況  
2025-2030 年全球 AI 晶片以 CAGR 32% 成長， AI/HPC 為主要動能 
近年 AI 應用擴大，初期 AI cloud 端訓練、推論採用通用型 GPU 為最大宗。隨 5G/AI/HPC 需求強
勁、晶圓代工廠持續往更先進製程邁進，未來將會有越來越多的 CSP/系統廠追求差異化、成本、
功耗考量而出現差異化晶片或自研晶片的需求，ASIC 應運而生，本中心預期隨著 ASIC 市場成長
加速，將帶動設計服務商營運。根據研調機構 Grand View Research，全球 AI 加速器市場將於
2030 年達約 2500 億美元，2025~30 年 CAGR 可達 32%，其中 ASIC CAGR 約 39%。由於
AI/HPC 晶片通常採用先進製程，其複雜性、生產時程及成本結構皆較適合採用 ASIC，此趨勢將推
動設計服務商成為 AI/HPC 晶片發展要角。根據供應鏈檢查，ASIC 對 CoWoS 的晶圓需求總量預
計將在 2026 年成長 85%，2027 年成長 71%，主要由 Google 的 TPU 以及 AWS 的 Trainium 系列
帶動成長。 
圖 7：AI 晶片市場 2025-30 年 CAGR 為 32% 圖 8：預計 2026/2027 年，ASIC 對 CoWoS 
的需求將增長 85%/71%

資料來源：公司資料、Grand View Research、元大投顧 資料來源：元大投顧預估

AI 晶片依功能可分為訓練/推論晶片 
AI 晶片在雲端進行訓練與推論，而在裝置端則著重推論功能，訓練晶片主要指通過大量資料輸入，
構建複雜深度學習類神經網路模型的 AI 晶片，運算能力較強；推論晶片主要是指利用訓練出來的
模型載入資料，計算並推理出各種結論的 AI 晶片。雲端與裝置端之算力需求有別，雲端的 AI 晶片
算力都要求在 30 TOPS 以上，而裝置端或邊緣端的 AI 晶片算力則在 5~30 TOPS 之間。本中心預
期隨 AI 持續發展，邊緣端 AI 應用的數量將不斷增加，帶動邊緣端 AI 晶片的需求持續上升，因此
在功耗、成本、大量生產的考量下，FPGA、ASIC 需求也將同步提升。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：IC 設計 2026 年 11 月 9 日 Alchip 第6 頁，共 19頁  
圖 9：不同部署位置之 AI 晶片算力要求 圖 10：深度學習之訓練與推論示意圖

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧 資料來源：公司資料、NVIDIA、元大投顧 
訓練端 AI 晶片以 GPU 為主流 
從技術架構來看 CPU、GPU 是較為成熟的通用型 AI 晶片，FPGA 和 ASIC 則是針對需求特化的半
訂製和全訂製晶片。因 AI 模型要求並行計算大量非結構化資料，CPU 在設計上以多工邏輯運算為
主要考量，較為適合處理複雜且不同類型之數據，而 GPU 則較為適合數據類型較為統一之邏輯運
算。GPU 構造相對單純，有數量眾多的計算單元與流水線，特別適合處理大量、類型統一的數據，
因此較為適合進行 AI 模型開發，預期 GPU 仍將是近年 AI 晶片架構之主流。

圖 11：CPU 與計算型 GPU 架構示意圖

資料來源：公司資料、元大投顧

ASIC 將應用在裝置/邊緣端之推論 AI 晶片 
FPGA 優勢在於可隨著 AI 模型改變而進行調整，在產品出廠後仍可對其進行編程，以執行不同的
功能，這使得 FPGA 成為產品原型設計、驗證想法和少量生產的完美選擇但因價格昂貴，算力遠
低於 GPU，功耗表現、效能、成本皆不如 ASIC，故 FPGA 可視為 AI 晶片從 GPU 轉至 ASIC 中
間的過渡方案。ASIC 在設計初期就已限定將使用何種 AI 模型，ASIC 晶片設計時間較長，通常需
要 2~3 年，但是在生產成本、專用度、功耗等皆優於 GPU、FPGA，因此 GPU 主要用於雲端的訓
練、推論功能，而 ASIC 晶片多用於雲端或裝置端、邊緣端的推論 AI 晶片。

AI 算力要求提高，訓練成本與耗時成為最大挑戰 
觀察現今 AI 產業對於算力的要求，其所需的算力提升遠高於摩爾定律，2012 年以來至今，AI 訓練
任務要求之算力每 3.43 個月便翻倍，大大超越晶片產業長期存在的摩爾定律 (每 18 個月晶片的性
能翻一倍)。因此 AI 訓練成本及消耗時間成新進者最大瓶頸，OpenAI 目前之 GPT-5 模型，訓練的
參數預估高達 2 兆個 (為 GPT-4 的 2 倍)，訓練成本可能來到 6,120 萬美元，若不考慮參數蒐集及
整理時間，光訓練人工智慧判讀資料，至少要花費 3-6 個月不等。

圖 13：人工智慧訓練成本高昂 圖 14：AI 模型訓練成本趨勢

資料來源：公司資料、The Next Platform、元大投顧 資料來源：公司資料、DataComony、元大投顧

ASIC 有效降低晶片單位生產成本、縮短製造時程 
ASIC 具計算效率高、單位製造成本低之特性，相比於其他晶片(CPU/GPU/FPGA)，ASIC 在計算
效率及功耗上表現更為優異，單位製造成本也較低，然而初期開發成本較其他標準品高。隨著製程
技術不斷演進，投資成本也急遽增加，IC 設計公司對於採用先進製程的產品開發上也傾向分工，將
後段晶片布局與實現交由專業的設計服務業者，如此不僅能有效降低研發成本，亦能縮短製造時程；
此外，近年來系統廠為了追求時效性與差異化，開始自行設計與規劃關鍵晶片，此亦為設計服務業
帶來龐大商機。
